- **CLQT** (arXiv:2606.29771): diagnostic benchmark that localizes where agent reasoning fails; most LLMs fail on consistency, not knowledge
- **BacktestBench** (arXiv:2605.17937): 18k QA pairs over 6M market records; LLMs score 70%+ on metrics but 45-65% on strategy selection and parameter tasks

- **Agentic Trading Survey** (arXiv:2605.19337): 0/19 studies reach R3 reproducibility
- **ReCAP** (arXiv:2606.00143): regime-adaptive continual learning with knowledge transfer on regime change
- **HMM+RL Allocation** (arXiv:2605.27848): RL policy over 3-state HMM achieves Sharpe 1.68 vs 0.92 static

# Browser Automation with agent-browser

## Quick start

```bash
agent-browser open <url>        # Navigate to page
agent-browser snapshot -i       # Get interactive elements with refs
agent-browser click @e1         # Click element by ref
agent-browser fill @e2 "text"   # Fill input by ref
agent-browser close             # Close browser
```

## Core workflow

1. Navigate: `agent-browser open <url>`
2. Snapshot: `agent-browser snapshot -i` (returns elements with refs like `@e1`, `@e2`)
3. Interact using refs from the snapshot
4. Re-snapshot after navigation or significant DOM changes

## Commands

### Navigation

```bash
agent-browser open <url>      # Navigate to URL
agent-browser back            # Go back
agent-browser forward         # Go forward
agent-browser reload          # Reload page
agent-browser close           # Close browser
```

### Snapshot (page analysis)

```bash
agent-browser snapshot            # Full accessibility tree
agent-browser snapshot -i         # Interactive elements only (recommended)
agent-browser snapshot -c         # Compact output
agent-browser snapshot -d 3       # Limit depth to 3
agent-browser snapshot -s "#main" # Scope to CSS selector
```

### Interactions (use @refs from snapshot)

```bash
agent-browser click @e1           # Click
agent-browser dblclick @e1        # Double-click
agent-browser fill @e2 "text"     # Clear and type
agent-browser type @e2 "text"     # Type without clearing
agent-browser press Enter         # Press key
agent-browser hover @e1           # Hover
agent-browser check @e1           # Check checkbox
agent-browser uncheck @e1         # Uncheck checkbox
agent-browser select @e1 "value"  # Select dropdown option
agent-browser scroll down 500     # Scroll page
agent-browser upload @e1 file.pdf # Upload files
```

### Get information

```bash
agent-browser get text @e1        # Get element text
agent-browser get html @e1        # Get inner...

<result>Both commits succeeded. Here is a summary of what was done:

**Dream cycle 2026-07-13 complete.** Two commits made.

**Research found:** Three strong papers from July 13, 2026 (all published today, so maximally fresh):

1. **arXiv:2607.11881 — Metacognition in LLMs** (Yale/UCI): First comprehensive survey of LLM self-awareness. Key finding: aggregate calibration statistics do not transfer to per-question accuracy (individuated calibration fails), which undermines confidence-weighted routing in multi-agent systems like H381/H382.

2. **arXiv:2607.11871 — Inside the Unfair Judge** (ICIR): LLM-as-judge scoring bias is not input-output noise — it lives in a low-dimensional subspace of the judge's activation geometry. Causal steering along that subspace reproduces or removes bias. Linear projection onto bias-direction features predicts judge failures on unseen benchmarks. Directly relevant to dream cycle hypothesis evaluation and H274/H381 bull/bear debate architectures.

3. **arXiv:2607.11399 — Agentic Routing: Harness-Native Data Flywheel** (Huawei Noah's Ark Lab): Step-level model routing inside agent harnesses, where each routing decision automatically generates labeled training records (no human annotation needed). The flywheel self-improves. OpenSquilla open-source implementation with LightGBM cold-start ranker. H318 meta-learner trading analog noted.

**Wiki changes:**
- 3 new pages created under `/workspace/agent/wiki/tools/` (llm-metacognition-2026.md, llm-judge-bias-2026.md, agentic-routing-2026.md)
- General Tech section grew from 7 to 10 entries
- Index page count updated 204 → 207, date updated to 2026-07...
